# Commands · L · part 4

1,447 entries, lit to logs. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lit**: Alias for the `literature-review` skill: Run a literature review using paper search and primary-source synthesis. ([cameronbracken/waterology-research](https://github.com/cameronbracken/waterology-research/blob/HEAD/commands/lit.md))
- **lit**: Paper-focused literature review using bioRxiv, Clinical Trials, and academic search ([emmahyde/dotfiles](https://github.com/emmahyde/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/cache/feynman-research/feynman/1.0.0/commands/lit.md))
- **lit**: Find prior art on a topic and report only what is missing from the research vault. ([namitdeb739/research-assistant](https://github.com/namitdeb739/research-assistant/blob/HEAD/commands/lit.md))
- **lit**: Literature search scoped to the current experiment domain — find papers, SOTA baselines, and related work without leaving the terminal. ([ThePyProgrammer/turing](https://github.com/ThePyProgrammer/turing/blob/HEAD/commands/lit.md))
- **lit-adhd**: Six-line verdict card after a break: what changed, the one thing to do now, what to type; 자세히/detail for the board, recap for sessions. ([wjgoarxiv/litclaude](https://github.com/wjgoarxiv/litclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/litclaude/commands/lit-adhd.md) · ★5)
- **lit-arc**: Compose a research literature **lineage arc** for `&lt;topic&gt;` — a structured narrative backed by a citation-graph corpus, per-paper Claude-read summaries, and structured citation statistics. The output length and section taxonomy are controlled by the ``--scope`` flag (default: 3-paragraph history/development/sota; can scale up to full 10-section review-paper structure). ([bobbyni819/vaultlab](https://github.com/bobbyni819/vaultlab/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lit-arc.md) · ★1)
- **lit-arc-next**: Reads the current project's `decisions-log.md` and prior arcs' "open questions" sections, then asks YOU (Claude Code, as the LLM) to propose the next-best topics to run `/lit-arc` on. ([bobbyni819/vaultlab](https://github.com/bobbyni819/vaultlab/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lit-arc-next.md) · ★1)
- **lit-brainstorm**: Brainstorm options for an open question, converge on a scored short list, then hand the pick to deep-interview or lit-plan. ([wjgoarxiv/litclaude](https://github.com/wjgoarxiv/litclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/litclaude/commands/lit-brainstorm.md) · ★5)
- **lit-compare**: Compare literature sources. ([HiraethEcho/AiKit](https://github.com/HiraethEcho/AiKit/blob/HEAD/modules/paper/commands/lit-compare.md))

- **lit-handoff**: Create or refresh a verified LitClaude continuation packet from the complete bundled handoff source. ([wjgoarxiv/litclaude](https://github.com/wjgoarxiv/litclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/litclaude/commands/lit-handoff.md) · ★5)

- **ll-agent**: One verb to drive the agent toolchain. Routes to /ll-agent-build, /ll-agent-eval, /ll-agent-deploy based on the subcommand. Default - if `path` exists and has agent.json, runs eval+deploy; else builds. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-agent.md))
- **ll-agent-boost**: WASM-based simple task executor — skips LLM calls entirely for deterministic code transforms at sub-millisecond speed ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-agent-boost.md))
- **ll-agent-build**: Scaffold a full production AI agent with planner + executor + RAG + memory + MCP tool layer + verifier + guardrails + human-in-the-loop + audit log + OpenTelemetry. Implements the autonomy-levels architecture from the blog. Output is a runnable repo, not just a prompt. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-agent-build.md))
- **ll-agent-call**: Call an agent (built by /ll-agent-build) as a Luna Pipes verb. Streams the agent's trace back into the pipe so downstream stages can react to its output. Lets agents be composed like any other verb — pipe in input, pipe out structured result. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-agent-call.md))
- **ll-agent-chain**: Chain multiple agents together — RAG + Nexa + OpenHands + LAM in custom pipelines ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-agent-chain.md))
- **ll-agent-deploy**: Deploy an agent scaffolded by /ll-agent-build to Cloudflare Workers, AWS Lambda, Google Cloud Run, Kubernetes, or Docker Compose. Provisions managed dependencies (Postgres, Redis, vector store) per the stack. Idempotent. Refuses to deploy if /ll-agent-eval has not passed. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-agent-deploy.md))
- **ll-agent-eval**: Run an agent's golden test suite, score task completion / tool-call efficiency / cost / latency / hallucination rate. Pipes through /ll-no-bluf to verify the agent isn't claiming work it didn't do. Required gate before /ll-agent-deploy. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-agent-eval.md))
- **ll-agent-swarm**: Spawn N variants of an agent in isolated git worktrees, run them in parallel on the same input, then pick the best result by consensus, by verifier score, or by lowest cost. Built on /ll-swarm. Use for ambiguous tasks where you want diverse first attempts before committing. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-agent-swarm.md))

- **ll-cve-doctor**: Defensive CVE scanner. Inventories the host (kernel version, libc, container runtime), the repo (lockfiles across npm, pip, go.mod, Cargo.toml, Maven, Gemfile, composer.lock), and (optionally) live K8s nodes. Cross-references against published CVE feeds (NVD, GitHub Advisories, OSV) and produces a prioritised remediation plan. Read-only and defense-only — does not generate exploits. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-cve-doctor.md))
- **ll-cve-triage**: Defense-only disclosure-triage workflow. Takes a CVE advisory (ID, URL, or pasted text), parses the affected ecosystems / packages / version ranges / kernel ranges, scans every project in scope (single repo, monorepo, or GitHub org), identifies which projects are actually affected, drafts an upgrade ticket per project (GitHub / Jira / Linear), and optionally opens upgrade PRs. Never generates or fetches exploit code. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-cve-triage.md))

- **ll-svelte-doctor**: Run `npx svelte-doctor-cli@latest` (or `svelte-doctor` if preferred) against the current repo to diagnose state, effects, performance, security, accessibility, architecture, and dead-code issues, then route each finding through `/ll-no-bluf` and open a fix-PR. - **ll-swarm**: Spawn a coordinated multi-agent swarm — parallel coding, reviewing, testing, and deploying with consensus ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm.md))
- **ll-swarm-blackboard**: Scaffold a Blackboard-pattern swarm — every agent reads and writes a typed shared case file (Postgres-backed) instead of passing chat history. Implements pattern §4.5 from blog/swarm-of-agents.md. Use when token cost of message-passing dominates and the case state is structured. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm-blackboard.md))
- **ll-swarm-debate**: Scaffold a Debate/Review/Verifier-pattern swarm — one agent proposes, a critic challenges, a verifier validates evidence and safety before the final answer. Implements pattern §4.4 from blog/swarm-of-agents.md. Recommended for fintech, security, billing, and any production-action context. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm-debate.md))
- **ll-swarm-supervisor**: Scaffold a Supervisor/Manager-pattern swarm — supervisor agent picks the next specialist, specialists return artifacts, verifier gates the final output, executor runs deterministic approved actions only. The safest production pattern from blog/swarm-of-agents.md §4.1. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm-supervisor.md))

- **llm-externalizer-discover-new-models**: Autodiscover models that newly appeared in the OpenRouter catalog since the last run — free: makes NO LLM call (no token cost), only a public model-catalog fetch (no API key). Diffs the live catalog against a seeded snapshot and, for each NEW model id, assesses it against every LLM tool's per-tool requirements so you can see at a glance which arrivals are worth adopting. Adopting one is a single scripted command (`--adopt`) — no hand-editing. Trigger with "any new models", "discover new models", "what new models are available", "check for newer/cheaper models". ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-discover-new-models.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-fix-found-bugs**: Aggregate unfixed findings across every report in `./reports/llm-externalizer/` and fix each via a fresh serial-fixer subagent (sonnet/opus menu). Optional `@merged-report.md` scopes the loop to one report. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-fix-found-bugs.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-fix-report**: Fix findings in ONE existing per-file scan report. Pick sonnet or opus via menu, dispatches a single parallel-fixer subagent, returns its `.fixer.`-summary path. For whole-folder audits use `/llm-externalizer:llm-externalizer-scan-and-fix`. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-fix-report.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-high-quality-scan**: Folder scan driven by ONE strong remote model (default z-ai/glm-5.2) at max reasoning effort + prompt cache, NOT the cheap 3-model ensemble. Thin wrapper over the high-quality-scan CLI command. Paid and OpenRouter-only — fails fast (never silently downgrades) on a local backend, free_only mode, or exhausted credit. Use when review quality matters more than cost. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-high-quality-scan.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-high-quality-scan-and-fix**: High-quality two-stage audit. ONE strong remote model (default z-ai/glm-5.2, max reasoning + cache) scans each file via high_quality_scan; then parallel Opus fixer subagents (≤15) verify and fix each finding in the same run. Paid + OpenRouter-only — fails fast on a local backend, free_only, or no credit. Orchestrator never reads scan or fixer content, only report paths. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-high-quality-scan-and-fix.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-install-statusline**: Install the LLM Externalizer multi-tier Claude Code statusline (model + context bar + cumulative CLI tokens/cost + OpenRouter credits + 5h/7d limits, with width-aware tiering and per-section error isolation). ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-install-statusline.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-mass-scout**: Mass-scouting dispatcher — every action of the pipeline in ONE command. Bare invocation runs the end-to-end LLM scout (phase 4: fieldset -&gt; JSON Schema, worker-pool fan-out, repair + validate, SQLite persist, markdown report). Pass an action word for everything else (register, estimate, search, export, diff, chain, ...); each action maps 1:1 to a hyphenated CLI subcommand, e.g. register -&gt; `bin/llm-ext mass-scout-register`. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-mass-scout.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-reset**: Reload settings.yaml, clear caches, and zero the on-disk session counters. Use after editing settings or when the backend misbehaves. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-reset.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-scan-and-fix**: Two-stage codebase audit. LLM Externalizer scan produces one report per file; parallel sonnet- or opus-model fixer subagents (≤15 concurrent) verify and fix each finding. Orchestrator never reads scan or fixer content — only report paths. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-scan-and-fix.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-scan-and-fix-serially**: Scan a codebase, aggregate findings into one canonical bug list, then fix each bug serially with a sonnet- or opus-model serial-fixer subagent. Use when fixes mutate shared state or bug order matters. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-scan-and-fix-serially.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-search-existing-benchmark**: Qualify OpenRouter model(s) for the search_existing_implementations task (duplicate-implementation match / "does this already exist?") against a labeled GOLDEN FIXTURE codebase, scored DETERMINISTICALLY by driving the REAL search-existing pipeline — precision/recall/F1 over the known duplicate locations, NO LLM judge. Recommends the best SAME-OR-CHEAPER model that PASSES (micro-F1 + micro-recall + coverage floors). A pricier model is NEVER auto-selected. Trigger with "benchmark the search-existing model", "qualify a model for search_existing_implementations", or "is model X good enough for du… ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-search-existing-benchmark.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-search-existing-implementations**: Scan a codebase (same language as the input files) for an existing implementation of a described feature. FFD-batched ensemble calls, exhaustive per-file output. Works for PR duplicate-check and greenfield audits. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-search-existing-implementations.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-security-scan**: Dedicated, injection-hardened security triage for suspected-malicious code. NOT the mass_scout pipeline — a bespoke judge with a nonce-delimited untrusted-data envelope, hardened system prompt, strict json_schema output, in-band injection pre-scan, and fail-safe-to- 'uncertain' on every error/deviation. Adjudicates a batch of targets (snippet | file_path+line+context_lines window | path_glob) into per-item verdicts and writes a JSON + markdown report. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-security-scan.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-security-triage-benchmark**: Qualify OpenRouter model(s) for the security_scan triage task against a labeled GOLDEN DATASET, scored through the REAL judge pipeline (same injection-hardened prompt, strict json_schema, clamp, fail-safe). Recommends the best SAME-OR-CHEAPER model that PASSES — zero under-flags on the critical judge-manipulation + visible-taint cases AND an aggregate at or above the calibrated floor. A pricier model is NEVER auto-selected. Trigger with "benchmark the triage model", "qualify a model for security_scan", or "is model X good enough for security triage". ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-security-triage-benchmark.md) · ★1)
- **llm-externalizer-setup**: Interactive setup wizard for the llm-externalizer plugin's local-model backends. - **llm-externalizer-update-all**: The WHOLE model refresh in one command — discover the live OpenRouter catalog, requirement-gate every tool, run every benchmark, rank, and write the winners (ensemble + per-tool models + the free_models pool) to settings.yaml. Hard spend cap; defaults to a provably $0 free-model refresh. Trigger with "update all the models", "refresh everything", "full model update", "update the model config", "search for free models", "rescan and apply", "update models and benchmarks". This ONE command IS the entire pipeline — route here instead of chaining the individual benchmark commands. ([Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin](https://github.com/Emasoft/llm-externalizer-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/llm-externalizer-update-all.md) · ★1)
